
  src\gameEngine.ts:9926:      x: Math.round(newHolder.pos.x * 10) / 10, z: Math.round(newHolder.pos.z * 10) / 10,
  src\gameEngine.ts:9927:    });
  src\gameEngine.ts:9928:    // Turnover debit: losing team's last holder loses a turnover
  src\gameEngine.ts:9929:    if (ballState.lastHeldBy && ballState.lastHeldBy.team === lostTeam) {
  src\gameEngine.ts:9930:      // Only count if not already counted by tackle (tackles add turnovers inline)
  src\gameEngine.ts:9931:      // Skip — tackle-triggered turnovers already credited at the tackle site
  src\gameEngine.ts:9932:    }
  src\gameEngine.ts:9933:
  src\gameEngine.ts:9934:    refreshDynamicTeamShape(elapsed, true);

  src\gameEngine.ts:9936:
  src\gameEngine.ts:9937:  function buildDefenseAssignments(defendingTeamId: 'A' | 'B', attackingTeamId: 'A' | 'B') {
  src\gameEngine.ts:9938:    const unassignedAttackers = getOrderedTeamPlayers(attackingTeamId).filter((p: any) => !isGoalkeeper(p));
  src\gameEngine.ts:9939:    const defenders = getOrderedTeamPlayers(defendingTeamId);
  src\gameEngine.ts:9940:    return defenders.map((player) => {
  src\gameEngine.ts:9941:      const profile = getDefensiveRoleProfile(player);
  src\gameEngine.ts:9942:      let markTarget = null;
  src\gameEngine.ts:9943:
  src\gameEngine.ts:9944:      if (unassignedAttackers.length > 0) {
  src\gameEngine.ts:9945:        unassignedAttackers.sort((a, b) => player.homePos.distanceTo(a.homePos) - player.homePos.distanceTo(b.homePos));
  src\gameEngine.ts:9946:        markTarget = unassignedAttackers.shift() || null;
  src\gameEngine.ts:9947:      }
  src\gameEngine.ts:9948:
  src\gameEngine.ts:9949:      return {
  src\gameEngine.ts:9950:        player,
  src\gameEngine.ts:9951:        markTarget,
  src\gameEngine.ts:9952:        ...profile
  src\gameEngine.ts:9953:      };
  src\gameEngine.ts:9954:    });
  src\gameEngine.ts:9955:  }
  src\gameEngine.ts:9956:
  src\gameEngine.ts:9957:  // Reset all players' homePos to their proper tactical formation positions
  src\gameEngine.ts:9958:  // so that restart-specific positioning (e.g. throw-in near corner) doesn't
  src\gameEngine.ts:9959:  // stick as the positional anchor during live play.
  src\gameEngine.ts:9960:  function resetHomePositions() {
  src\gameEngine.ts:9961:    // Determine which team currently has possession for dynamic homePos shifting
  src\gameEngine.ts:9962:    const possTeam: 'A' | 'B' | null =
  src\gameEngine.ts:9963:      ballState.heldBy?.team === 'A' || ballState.heldBy?.team === 'B'
  src\gameEngine.ts:9964:        ? ballState.heldBy.team
  src\gameEngine.ts:9965:        : (ballState.lastHeldBy?.team === 'A' || ballState.lastHeldBy?.team === 'B')
  src\gameEngine.ts:9966:          ? ballState.lastHeldBy.team
  src\gameEngine.ts:9967:          : null;
  src\gameEngine.ts:9968:
  src\gameEngine.ts:9969:    for (const team of ['A', 'B'] as const) {
  src\gameEngine.ts:9970:      const attackRight = teamAttacksRight(team);
  src\gameEngine.ts:9971:      const attackSign = attackRight ? 1 : -1;
  src\gameEngine.ts:9972:      const formation = SOCCER_FORMATIONS[activeTeamFormations[team]] || SOCCER_FORMATIONS['4-2-3-1'];
  src\gameEngine.ts:9973:      const teamPlayers = getOrderedTeamPlayers(team);
  src\gameEngine.ts:9974:      const hasPossession = possTeam === team;
  src\gameEngine.ts:9975:
  src\gameEngine.ts:9976:      // How far forward the ball is in this team's attack direction (0 = own goal, 1 = opponent goal)
  src\gameEngine.ts:9977:      const ballProgress = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(
  src\gameEngine.ts:9978:        ((ballState.pos.x * attackSign) + FIELD_W / 2) / FIELD_W, 0, 1
  src\gameEngine.ts:9979:      );
  src\gameEngine.ts:9980:
  src\gameEngine.ts:9981:      for (let i = 0; i < teamPlayers.length && i < formation.team.length; i++) {
  src\gameEngine.ts:9982:        const slot = toSoccerFieldPosition(formation.team[i], attackRight);
  src\gameEngine.ts:9983:        let homeX = slot.x;
  src\gameEngine.ts:9984:
  src\gameEngine.ts:9985:        // Dynamic possession shift: move homePos toward ball during possession
  src\gameEngine.ts:9986:        // so the entire team compresses forward with the attack
  src\gameEngine.ts:9987:        if (hasPossession && ballProgress > 0.35) {
  src\gameEngine.ts:9988:          const role = (teamPlayers[i].displayRole || teamPlayers[i].role || '').toString().toUpperCase();
  src\gameEngine.ts:9989:          let shiftWeight = 0;
  src\gameEngine.ts:9990:          switch (role) {
  src\gameEngine.ts:9991:            case 'GK': shiftWeight = 0.0;  break;
  src\gameEngine.ts:9992:            case 'CB': shiftWeight = 0.18; break;
  src\gameEngine.ts:9993:            case 'LB': case 'RB': shiftWeight = 0.28; break;
  src\gameEngine.ts:9994:            case 'DM': shiftWeight = 0.32; break;
  src\gameEngine.ts:9995:            case 'CM': shiftWeight = 0.42; break;
  src\gameEngine.ts:9996:            case 'LM': case 'RM': shiftWeight = 0.48; break;
  src\gameEngine.ts:9997:            case 'ST': shiftWeight = 0.50; break;
  src\gameEngine.ts:9998:            default:   shiftWeight = 0.25; break;
  src\gameEngine.ts:9999:          }
  src\gameEngine.ts:10000:          // Scale shift by how deep the ball is (no shift in own half, full shift in final third)
  src\gameEngine.ts:10001:          const depthFactor = THREE.MathUtils.clamp((ballProgress - 0.35) / 0.55, 0, 1);
  src\gameEngine.ts:10002:          homeX = THREE.MathUtils.lerp(homeX, ballState.pos.x, shiftWeight * depthFactor);
  src\gameEngine.ts:10003:        }
  src\gameEngine.ts:10004:
  src\gameEngine.ts:10005:        // ── Score-aware shape: winning team sits deeper, losing team pushes higher ──
  src\gameEngine.ts:10006:        const ownGoals = team === 'A' ? gameState.scoreA : gameState.scoreB;
  src\gameEngine.ts:10007:        const oppGoals = team === 'A' ? gameState.scoreB : gameState.scoreA;
  src\gameEngine.ts:10008:        const goalDiff = ownGoals - oppGoals;
  src\gameEngine.ts:10009:        if (goalDiff !== 0 && !isGoalkeeper(teamPlayers[i])) {
  src\gameEngine.ts:10010:          // Positive goalDiff = winning → shift toward own goal; negative = losing → shift toward opp goal
  src\gameEngine.ts:10011:          const ownGoalX = attackSign > 0 ? -FIELD_W / 2 : FIELD_W / 2;
  src\gameEngine.ts:10012:          const shiftMagnitude = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(Math.abs(goalDiff), 0, 3) * 2.5;
  src\gameEngine.ts:10013:          const direction = goalDiff > 0 ? 1 : -1; // 1 = toward own goal, -1 = toward opp goal
  src\gameEngine.ts:10014:          homeX += (ownGoalX - homeX > 0 ? 1 : -1) * direction * shiftMagnitude * 0.12;
  src\gameEngine.ts:10015:        }
  src\gameEngine.ts:10016:
  src\gameEngine.ts:10017:        teamPlayers[i].homePos.set(homeX, 0.15, slot.z);
  src\gameEngine.ts:10018:      }
  src\gameEngine.ts:10019:    }
  src\gameEngine.ts:10020:  }
  src\gameEngine.ts:10021:
  src\gameEngine.ts:10022:  function refreshDynamicTeamShape(elapsed: number, force: boolean = false) {
  src\gameEngine.ts:10023:    const liveOffenseTeam: 'A' | 'B' = (ballState.heldBy?.team === 'A' || ballState.heldBy?.team === 'B')
  src\gameEngine.ts:10024:      ? ballState.heldBy.team
  src\gameEngine.ts:10025:      : ((ballState.lastHeldBy?.team === 'A' || ballState.lastHeldBy?.team === 'B')

  src\gameEngine.ts:10027:        : gameState.offensiveTeam);
  src\gameEngine.ts:10028:    gameState.offensiveTeam = liveOffenseTeam;
  src\gameEngine.ts:10029:
  src\gameEngine.ts:10030:    const defendingTeam: 'A' | 'B' = liveOffenseTeam === 'A' ? 'B' : 'A';
  src\gameEngine.ts:10031:    const shouldRefresh =
  src\gameEngine.ts:10032:      force ||
  src\gameEngine.ts:10033:      playState.dynamicOffenseTeam !== liveOffenseTeam ||
  src\gameEngine.ts:10034:      elapsed - playState.lastShapeRefresh > 0.22 ||
  src\gameEngine.ts:10035:      playState.activeRoutes.length === 0 ||
  src\gameEngine.ts:10036:      playState.defenseAssignments.length === 0;
  src\gameEngine.ts:10037:    if (!shouldRefresh) return;
  src\gameEngine.ts:10038:
  src\gameEngine.ts:10039:    offenseTeam.length = 0;
  src\gameEngine.ts:10040:    defenseTeam.length = 0;
  src\gameEngine.ts:10041:    getOrderedTeamPlayers(liveOffenseTeam).forEach((p: any) => offenseTeam.push(p));
  src\gameEngine.ts:10042:    getOrderedTeamPlayers(defendingTeam).forEach((p: any) => defenseTeam.push(p));

  src\gameEngine.ts:10047:    playState.dynamicOffenseTeam = liveOffenseTeam;
  src\gameEngine.ts:10048:    playState.lastShapeRefresh = elapsed;
  src\gameEngine.ts:10049:  }
